A purposive audit of 10 papers across four methodological strands finds no dedicated identification-assumptions section and a recurring pattern: validation metrics such as faithfulness, completeness, monosemanticity, alignment, or ablation effects are reported as causal support without stating the assumptions that make them identifying. A two-human-coder audit on $n=30$ reproduces the direction of the main finding: dedicated identification sections are absent, and validation-metric substitution is common, though exact Dim B/D counts are coding-rule sensitive. The paper proposes a disclosure norm: state whether the claim is causal, name the identification strategy, enumerate assumptions, stress at least one, and explain how conclusions shift if assumptions fail. Validation is not identification.

</details>


### [290] [Bifurcation Models: Learning Set-Valued Solution Maps with Weight-Tied Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07277)
*Caleb Jore,Jialin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分岔模型（bifurcation models），通过权重共享的动态视角，使不同初始条件收敛到不同稳定平衡点，从而表示吸引子景观而非单一分支。理论证明了具有局部Lipschitz分支的集值映射可由正则平衡动力学表示，且诱导选择器几乎处处正则。在 frustrated Ising 模型和 Allen-Cahn 实验上验证了该方法无需分支标签即可发现多个有效平衡点，并揭示了准确率与多样性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与组合问题存在多个正确解，但标准监督学习通过人为选择一个解作为目标来消除歧义，这个隐藏的选择器可能具有任意性、不连续性且比底层解集更难学习。

Method: 提出分岔模型（bifurcation models），采用权重共享的动态视角，利用不同初始化收敛到不同稳定平衡点的特性，将模型表示为吸引子景观而非单一选择分支。

Result: 在 frustrated Ising 模型上，该方法无需分支标签即可发现多个有效平衡点，优于单分支监督方法；Allen-Cahn 实验表明多样性需要显式鼓励，且存在准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 权重共享的动态模型能够自然地表示多解问题中的解集，避免了人为选择器带来的学习困难，但需要谨慎处理多样性与准确性之间的平衡。

Abstract: Many scientific and combinatorial problems admit multiple correct solutions, not a single label. Standard supervised learning resolves this ambiguity by choosing one solution as the target, but this hidden selector can be arbitrary, discontinuous, and harder to learn than the underlying solution set. We study bifurcation models, a weight-tied dynamical view in which different initializations can converge to different stable equilibria, so the model represents an attractor landscape rather than one chosen branch. We prove that broad set-valued maps with locally Lipschitz branches can be represented by regular equilibrium dynamics and that the induced selectors are almost everywhere regular, while manual selectors can be arbitrarily irregular. Experiments on frustrated Ising models show that such dynamics can discover multiple valid equilibria without branch labels and outperform single-branch supervision. Allen--Cahn experiments further show that diversity is not automatic: it can be encouraged explicitly, but with an accuracy--diversity tradeoff.

</details>


### [291] [Predictive but Not Plannable: RC-aux for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07278)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-aux辅助目标，通过在时间和空间轴上添加规划对齐的监督信号，修正潜在世界模型中的时空不匹配问题，使潜空间更好地服务于长 horizon 规划任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型在短时预测上表现良好，但其潜空间与规划目标不对齐，尤其是欧氏距离无法反映有限动作预算下的可达性，导致长 horizon 目标导向搜索效果差。

Method: RC-aux包含两个辅助监督：1) 时间轴上，多 horizon 开环预测训练超越单步一致性；2) 空间轴上，预算条件化的可达性监督配合时间硬负样本，区分最终可达与当前规划 horizon 内可达的状态。测试时，学习到的可达性信号还可用于可达性感知规划器。

Result: 在目标条件像素控制任务和LIBERO-Goal扩展上，RC-aux以较小额外开销显著提升了LeWM-style规划性能。

Conclusion: 潜在世界模型的规划效果不仅取决于预测精度，还取决于表示是否编码了下游搜索所需的时间与几何结构。RC-aux为轻量级解决此不匹配问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: A latent world model may achieve accurate short-horizon prediction while still inducing a latent space that is poorly aligned with planning. A key issue is spatiotemporal mismatch: these models are often trained with local predictive supervision, but deployed for long-horizon goal-directed search in latent spaces where Euclidean distance may not reflect what is reachable within a finite action budget. We present the Reachability-Correction auxiliary objective (RC-aux), a lightweight correction for this mismatch in reconstruction-free latent world models. RC-aux keeps the world-model backbone unchanged and adds planning-aligned supervision along two axes. Along the time axis, multi-horizon open-loop prediction trains the model beyond one-step consistency. Along the space axis, budget-conditioned reachability supervision, together with temporal hard negatives, encourages the latent space to distinguish states that are eventually reachable from those reachable within the current planning horizon. At test time, the learned reachability signal can also be used by a reachability-aware planner to favor trajectories that are both goal-directed and attainable under the available budget. We instantiate RC-aux on LeWorldModel and evaluate it under both continuation-training and matched-from-scratch settings. Across goal-conditioned pixel-control tasks and a LIBERO-Goal extension, RC-aux improves LeWM-style planning with modest additional cost. These results suggest that planning with latent world models depends not only on predictive accuracy, but also on whether the learned representation encodes the temporal and geometric structure required by downstream search. The code is available at https://github.com/Guang000/RC-aux.

</details>


### [292] [Mask2Cause: Causal Discovery via Adjacency Constrained Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07280)
*Omar Muhammad,Pasupuleti Dhruv Shivkant,Deepak N. Subramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mask2Cause是一个端到端因果发现框架，通过引入倒置变量嵌入和邻接约束掩码注意力机制，在时间序列预测前向过程中直接恢复因果图，在多个基准上实现了最先进的因果发现性能，并能将预测模型参数减少70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经因果发现方法存在两个主要问题：一是基于组件架构（component-wise）无法捕获共享系统动态；二是采用解耦的后处理图提取方式容易过拟合到虚假相关性。

Method: 提出Mask2Cause端到端框架，包含两个核心创新：(1) 倒置变量嵌入（Inverted Variable Embedding）；(2) 邻接约束掩码注意力机制（Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention）。训练时采用同方差或异方差目标函数，以捕获均值和方差中的因果影响。

Result: 在合成混沌动力学和真实生物模拟等多个基准上取得最先进的因果发现效果，参数复杂度显著低于基线方法。推断出的因果结构可将预测模型参数平均减少70%以上，同时保持预测准确性。

Conclusion: Mask2Cause通过端到端学习因果图的方式，有效解决了现有方法的缺陷，在因果发现效率和模型压缩方面均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Leveraging deep learning for causal discovery in time series remains challenging because existing neural methods predominantly rely on component-wise architectures that fail to capture shared system dynamics or employ decoupled post-hoc graph extraction that risks overfitting to spurious correlations. We propose $\textbf{Mask2Cause}$, an end-to-end framework that recovers the underlying causal graph directly during the forecasting forward pass. Our approach introduces an Inverted Variable Embedding and an Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention mechanism, trained with homoscedastic or heteroscedastic objectives to capture causal influences in both mean and variance. Empirical results on diverse benchmarks, from synthetic chaotic dynamics to realistic biological simulations, demonstrate state-of-the-art causal discovery with significantly reduced parameter complexity compared to standard baselines. We further show that inferred causal structures can be used to reduce parameter count of forecasting models by more than 70% on average while maintaining predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [293] [Instruction Tuning Changes How Upstream State Conditions Late Readout: A Cross-Patching Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07284)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“首次分歧交叉修补”方法，研究预训练基础模型与指令微调模型在早期计算与晚期层如何协作产生输出差异。发现指令微调的晚期层效应依赖于自身早期状态，而非可移植的基础模型早期状态；最终MLP层稀疏特征部分中介了这一效应。建议定位到晚期层的差异研究应检验其在不同上游状态下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有可解释性工作将行为定位到模型中晚期层（如拒绝方向、助手指征轴等），但早期计算与晚期层如何协作将差异转化为下一个token的对数几率尚不清楚。本文旨在厘清这一协作机制。

Method: 提出“首次分歧交叉修补”（first-divergence cross-patching）：在预训练基础模型与指令微调模型首次预测token不一致的位置，将每个模型的早期层状态与每个模型的晚期层状态交叉替换，从而分离不同训练配方下早期与晚期层的贡献。

Result: 指令微调的晚期层从自身早期状态读取时效果更大（+2.44 logits），从基础模型早期状态读取时效果较小（+0.76 logits），交互效应为正（+1.68）。OpenMath2的数学领域SFT的晚期效应可移植自基础模型早期状态。最终MLP层稀疏特征部分中介此效应。强制token评分显示局部token选择可影响后续精确答案的成功率。

Conclusion: 晚期层并非自包含的功能模块，其效应依赖于上游早期层的状态。建议将行为差异定位到晚期层的研究应检验该效应在另一模型上游状态下是否仍然存在，以验证晚期层是否真正自包含。

Abstract: Recent interpretability work has identified model-internal handles on post-trained behavior, including refusal directions, assistant/persona axes, and sparse chat-tuning features. These results localize where behaviors can be read out or controlled, often in middle-to-late layers. We ask how earlier computation and the late stack cooperate to turn those differences into next-token margins. To test this, we introduce first-divergence cross-patching: at the first token where pretrained base (PT) and instruction-tuned (IT) checkpoints disagree, we cross each model's earlier-layer state with each model's late stack. The diagnostic separates training recipes: same-base instruction-following descendants show late effects that depend on their own earlier-layer state, while OpenMath2 math-domain SFT and controlled code/biomed CPT controls with verified domain learning do not; for OpenMath2, the late effect is already largely portable from base earlier-layer state. Across five dense families (4B-32B), the IT late stack adds +0.76 logits from PT upstream and +2.44 from IT upstream, giving a +1.68 interaction that is positive in every family. Thus the late stack has a real PT-upstream effect, but its larger effect in the IT checkpoint appears only when it reads its own post-trained upstream state. Sparse features in final MLP layers partially mediate the effect and are driven by upstream patches, supporting a handoff from earlier state to final-layer feature activation to IT-token margin. Forced-token scoring shows that the local token choice can change later exact-answer success. Operationally, paired-checkpoint studies that localize a difference to late layers should test whether it survives under the other checkpoint's upstream state before treating the late stack as self-contained.

</details>


### [294] [Pretraining Induces a Reusable Spectral Basis for Downstream Task Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07302)
*Junjie Yu,Yue Wang,Zihan Deng,Yan Zhu,Wenxiao Ma,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统谱分析发现，预训练模型微调时，权重矩阵的主导奇异向量在微调过程中保持高度稳定，且在不同下游任务间共享，表明预训练建立了可复用的谱坐标系。基于此提出参数高效方法，仅优化主导谱系数，在GLUE上使用0.2%可训练参数达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注微调时的优化子空间，但忽略了微调中某些方向为何不被探索的问题：这些稳定方向是与下游任务无关，还是已经编码了任务相关结构？回答这一问题对于理解预训练知识如何迁移至关重要。

Method: 对视觉和语言模型进行系统谱分析，研究预训练权重矩阵奇异向量在微调过程中的稳定性；提出参数高效微调方法，冻结预训练奇异向量，仅优化主导谱系数。

Result: 主导奇异向量在微调中高度稳定且跨无关下游任务共享；在更大数据集上预训练的模型展现出更强的谱稳定性；所提方法在GLUE上仅用0.2%可训练参数即可达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: 稳定方向编码的是可迁移的结构而非无关噪声；成功的预训练能够发现下游任务继承并运行的谱基。

Abstract: Finetuning pretrained models occurs in a low-dimensional subspace of the full parameter space. Prior work has focused on characterizing this optimization subspace, but largely ignored the complementary question: why do certain directions remain unexplored during finetuning? Are these stable directions irrelevant to downstream tasks, or do they already encode task-relevant structure that requires no further adjustment? Answering this question is central to understanding how pretrained knowledge transfers. Through systematic spectral analysis across vision and language models, we show that the leading singular vectors of pretrained weight matrices remain highly stable under finetuning and are shared across unrelated downstream tasks, revealing that pretraining establishes a reusable spectral coordinate system. Models pretrained on larger datasets exhibit greater spectral stability under distribution shift or task change, directly linking pretraining scale to geometric transferability. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient method that freezes pretrained singular vectors and optimizes only leading spectral coefficients, achieving competitive performance on GLUE with 0.2% trainable parameters. Our results reveal that the stable directions encode transferable structure rather than irrelevant noise: successful pretraining discovers spectral bases that downstream tasks inherit and operate within.

</details>


### [295] [Latent Order Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07304)
*Emil Carlsson,Newton Mwai,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在顺序赌博机（LOB），通过仅要求每个状态下动作偏好的部分顺序先验知识，放宽了传统潜在赌博机对精确后验分布和离散潜在状态的严格假设，使得同一状态的不同实例可以在共享动作偏好的同时具有不同的奖励分布，并设计了UCB和 posterior-sampling 两种算法，理论上证明了UCB的遗憾上界，实验表明两种算法在奖励参数共享场景下与传统方法相当，在奖励尺度不同时表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜在赌博机算法虽然能利用离散潜在状态的结构信息来减少探索，但要求后验分布精确已知且状态数足够描述所有实例的奖励分布，这在实践中难以满足。本文旨在放松这些严格假设，使得算法在仅需动作偏好的部分顺序先验知识时仍能有效工作，并适用于奖励分布不同但偏好顺序相同的场景。

Method: 提出潜在顺序赌博机（LOB）框架，假设每个潜在状态下动作存在部分顺序偏好的先验知识，同一状态的不同实例可具有不同奖励分布但共享该偏好顺序。设计了基于置信上界（UCB）的算法并给出遗憾上界证明，同时提出后验采样（posterior-sampling）算法以提升实证性能。

Result: 理论上给出了UCB算法在完全顺序和部分顺序两种情况下的遗憾上界分析。实验结果表明，所提LOB算法（UCB和后验采样）在相同状态实例共享奖励参数时与传统潜在赌博机竞争力相当，而在奖励尺度不同的场景下优于传统方法。

Conclusion: LOB通过仅需部分顺序先验而非完整奖励分布先验，有效扩展了潜在赌博机的适用性，在保持理论保证的同时提升了实际场景中的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Bandit algorithms solve diverse sequential decision-making problems, but are often too sample-inefficient for from-scratch personalization. To substantially reduce exploration times, latent bandit algorithms exploit cross-instance structure implied by discrete latent states, provided that the posterior distribution of rewards and latent states is known and accurate. However, obtaining an accurate model of this structure is difficult, and a small number of latent states may be insufficient to characterize the reward distributions in all problem instances. We propose latent order bandits (LOB), relaxing the assumptions of latent bandits to require only prior knowledge of a partial order of action preferences in each state. This allows instances of the same state to vary in reward distributions, as long as the partial order of actions is shared. For example, groups of users on a streaming service may agree on which movie genres are the best but rate experiences on different scales. We give an upper-confidence bound procedure for the LOB problem, applicable to both total and partial latent orders, and give an upper bound on its regret. To improve empirical performance, we propose a posterior-sampling algorithm and show, in a suite of experiments, that both are competitive with full-prior latent bandits when same-state instances share reward parameters, and preferable to them when reward scales differ between instances with the same latent state.

</details>


### [296] [Generative Modeling with Flux Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07319)
*Peter Pao-Huang,Xiaojie Qiu,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flux Matching 是一种新的生成模型范式，通过放宽得分匹配的约束条件，允许学习非保守向量场，从而获得更灵活、可设计的生成动力学模型，在图像生成、快速采样和可解释建模等方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于得分的生成模型要求模型等于数据得分（即保守向量场），这一强约束限制了模型的灵活性和可设计性。作者希望提出一种更宽松的学习目标，允许模型学习非保守向量场，从而能够直接注入归纳偏置、结构先验和动力学特性。

Method: 提出 Flux Matching 目标函数，该目标不要求模型等于数据得分，而是施加更弱的条件：只需模型的稳态分布等于数据分布。这允许存在无穷多个满足条件的向量场，从而拓展了可学习的生成模型空间。

Result: 在高维图像数据集上表现强劲；实现了更快的采样速度；能够学习可解释和机制性的动力学模型；能够编码变量间的有向依赖关系。这些应用在传统的得分匹配框架下无法实现。

Conclusion: Flux Matching 将向量场本身从固定目标转变为可设计的选择，开创了生成模型的新维度，为更灵活、更具可解释性的生成动力学建模提供了可能。

Abstract: We introduce Flux Matching, a new paradigm for generative modeling that generalizes existing score-based models to a broader family of vector fields that need not be conservative. Rather than requiring the model to equal the data score, the Flux Matching objective imposes a weaker condition that admits infinitely many vector fields whose stationary distribution is the data. This flexibility enables a class of generative models that cannot be learned under score matching, in which inductive biases, structural priors, and properties of the dynamics can be directly imposed or optimized. We show that Flux Matching performs strongly on high-dimensional image datasets and, more importantly, that our added freedom unlocks a range of applications including faster sampling, interpretable and mechanistic models, and dynamics that encode directed dependencies between variables. More broadly, Flux Matching opens a new dimension in generative modeling by turning the vector field itself into a design choice rather than a fixed target. Code is available at https://github.com/peterpaohuang/flux_matching.

</details>


### [297] [Rethinking Importance Sampling in LLM Policy Optimization: A Cumulative Token Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07331)
*Yuheng Zhang,Chenlu Ye,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Wei Xiong,Saurabh Sahu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习（RLVR）中重要性采样比率存在的偏差-方差困境，本文提出累积Token重要性采样比率作为理论上的最优解，并据此设计了CTPO（累积Token策略优化）方法，通过位置自适应裁剪实现更一致的跨位置正则化，在数学推理基准上取得了最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（PPO/GRPO的token级IS比率引入偏差，全序列IS比率方差过高，GSPO的长度归一化偏离精确校正）面临偏差-方差困境，需要一种既无偏又低方差的IS比率设计方案。

Method: 提出累积Token IS比率（位置t之前的逐token比率乘积），理论证明其在token级策略梯度公式中对每个token梯度项提供无偏前缀校正，且方差严格低于全序列比率。基于此设计CTPO，结合位置自适应裁剪（按log空间clip边界的√t增长进行缩放），实现更一致的跨位置正则化。

Result: 在工具集成推理设置下的多个挑战性数学推理基准上，CTPO在两个模型规模上均取得了优于GRPO和GSPO强基线的平均最佳性能。

Conclusion: 累积Token IS比率是解决RLVR中偏差-方差困境的理论上的最优方案，CTPO通过理论驱动的设计与实践验证，为LLM后训练中的策略优化提供了更有效的方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning, including reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), has emerged as a powerful approach for LLM post-training. Central to these approaches is the design of the importance sampling (IS) ratio used in off-policy policy-gradient estimation. Existing methods face a fundamental bias-variance dilemma: token-level IS ratios, as adopted by PPO (Schulman et al., 2017) and GRPO (Shao et al., 2024), introduce bias by ignoring prefix state distribution mismatch; full sequence ratios provide exact trajectory-level correction but suffer from high variance due to the multiplicative accumulation of per-token ratios, while GSPO (Zheng et al., 2025) improves numerical stability via length normalization at the cost of deviating from the exact full-sequence IS correction. In this work, we identify the cumulative token IS ratio, the product of per-token ratios up to position $t$, as a theoretically principled solution to this dilemma. We prove that, under the token-level policy-gradient formulation, this ratio provides an unbiased prefix correction for each token-level gradient term and has strictly lower variance than the full sequence ratio. Building on this insight, we propose CTPO (Cumulative Token Policy Optimization), which combines the cumulative token IS ratio with position-adaptive clipping that scales log-space clip bounds according to the natural $\sqrt{t}$ growth of the cumulative log-ratio. This yields more consistent regularization across token positions. We implement and evaluate CTPO in the tool-integrated reasoning setting on several challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, achieving the best average performance across both model scales compared with strong GRPO and GSPO baselines. Code will be available at https://github.com/horizon-llm/CTPO.

</details>


### [298] [Beyond Linear Attention: Softmax Transformers Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07333)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了使用标准softmax注意力的Transformer在上下文强化学习（ICRL）中的工作机制，证明了其前向传播等价于加权softmax时序差分（TD）学习算法的迭代更新，并证明了在特定参数下策略评估误差随层数增加而衰减，且这些参数是预训练损失的全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICRL的理论分析大多依赖线性注意力（用恒等映射替换softmax），这与实际使用的softmax注意力不符。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为实际应用中的softmax注意力提供理论依据。

Method: 论文证明：1）具有特定参数的softmax注意力Transformer的前向传播等价于加权softmax TD学习的迭代更新（一种在核空间进行策略评估的新算法，统一了线性TD和表格型TD）；2）在特定收缩条件下，策略评估误差随层数增加而衰减；3）这些参数是预训练损失的全局最小值。

Result: 1）建立了softmax注意力与加权softmax TD算法之间的等价性；2）证明了在收缩条件下策略评估误差的衰减性；3）证明识别出的参数是预训练损失的全局最小值，并通过数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文为实际使用的softmax注意力的ICRL提供了首个严格的理论分析，揭示了Transformer前向传播与核空间策略评估算法之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) studies agents that, after pretraining, adapt to new tasks by conditioning on additional context without parameter updates. Existing theoretical analyses of ICRL largely rely on linear attention, which replaces the softmax function in the standard attention with an identity mapping. This paper provides the first theoretical understanding of ICRL without making the unrealistic linear attention simplification. In particular, we consider the standard softmax attention used in practice. We show that, with certain parameters, the layerwise forward pass of a Transformer with such softmax attention is equivalent to iterative updates of a weighted softmax temporal difference (TD) learning algorithm. Here, weighted softmax TD is a new RL algorithm that performs policy evaluation in kernel space and adopts both linear TD and tabular TD as special cases. We also prove that under a certain contraction condition, the policy evaluation error decays as the number of layers grows, with the identified parameters above. Finally, we prove that those parameters are a global minimizer of a pretraining loss, explaining their emergence in our numerical experiments.

</details>


### [299] [CellScientist: Dual-Space Hierarchical Orchestration for Closed-Loop Refinement of Virtual Cell Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07335)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Jiaying Wang,Wenbin Xing,Yixuan Dong,Chengyang Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Yuzhong Peng,Jinlin Wu,Bob Zhang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Fuji Yang,Zhen Lei,Jiebo Luo,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CellScientist提出了一种双空间层级框架，将高层假设空间与低层可执行实现空间耦合，通过结构化反馈传播解决虚拟细胞建模中的精炼-路由问题，实现从执行失败到假设或实现更新的闭环迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助建模工作流存在精炼-路由问题：预测偏差通过可执行实现被观测到，但相关的修正可能涉及建模假设、表征设计、实现或任务约束等多个层面，缺乏结构化的反馈传播机制，导致迭代精炼可能只修复代码而未修正根本的假设错误。

Method: 提出CellScientist双空间层级框架，包含高层假设空间和低层可执行实现空间。将建模决策表示为结构化状态，在任务和接口约束下实现为可接受程序，并将执行偏差路由回目标假设或实现更新，形成假设→实现→假设的闭环。

Result: 在形态学和转录组学基准测试以及单细胞扰动评估中，CellScientist选择的最终可执行模型在固定分割和评估协议下优于参考基线，并且工作流生成可审计的精炼轨迹。

Conclusion: CellScientist通过结构化反馈传播将执行失败转化为模型精炼的结构化信号而非调试事件，有效解决了迭代精炼中假设与实现层面的对齐问题，为虚拟细胞建模提供了可审计、可追溯的精炼范式。

Abstract: Virtual Cell Modeling (VCM) requires models that not only predict perturbation responses, but also support targeted revision when predictions fail. Current LLM-assisted modeling workflows face a refinement-routing problem: prediction discrepancies are observed through executable implementations, but the relevant revision may involve the modeling assumption, representation design, implementation, or task constraint. Without structured feedback propagation across these levels, iterative refinement may repair code while failing to revise the assumption responsible for the discrepancy. We propose CellScientist, a dual-space hierarchical framework that couples a high-level hypothesis space with a low-level executable implementation space. CellScientist represents modeling decisions as structured states, realizes them as admissible programs under task and interface constraints, and routes execution discrepancies back to targeted hypothesis or implementation updates. This enables a closed Hypothesis -> Implementation -> Hypothesis loop where failures become structured signals for model refinement rather than debugging events. Across morphology and transcriptomic benchmarks, with additional single-cell perturbation evaluations, the final executable models selected by CellScientist improve over reference baselines under fixed split and evaluation protocols, while the workflow produces auditable refinement traces.

</details>


### [300] [Mage: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLM-Generated Executable Game Scenes Beyond Compile-Pass Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07342)
*Hugh Xuechen Liu,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 编译通过率在LLM代码生成中具有误导性；针对游戏场景合成，提出四轴评估协议Mage，发现编译通过率与功能正确性负相关，多轴评估必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译通过率作为LLM代码生成的主要评估信号具有误导性，尤其对多组件领域特定工件而言，需要更全面的评估方法。

Method: 提出四轴评估协议Mage（编译成功、运行时成功、结构保真度、机制遵循），对4种开源LLM（7B-30B）的858次生成尝试进行评估，比较直接NL-to-C#生成与结构IR条件生成。

Result: 直接NL-to-C#生成本运行时通过率最高（均值43%），但产生结构空洞场景（机制F1≈0.12）；结构IR条件生成运行时通过率减半，但恢复领域忠实结构（F1最高1.00）；行为级与全场景粒度在统计上无显著差异（McNemar p=1.0）。

Conclusion: 编译通过率与功能正确性在该领域呈负相关，多轴评估是检测这种偏离的必要手段。

Abstract: Compile-pass rate is the dominant evaluation signal for LLM code generation, yet for multi-component domain-specific artifacts it can be actively misleading. We demonstrate this on executable game scene synthesis with a four-axis evaluation protocol (named `Mage') -- compile success, runtime success, structural fidelity, and mechanism adherence -- applied to 858 generation attempts across four open-weight LLMs (7B--30B), 26~hand-crafted Unity goal pattern playable concepts, and two automatically extracted IR granularity levels. Direct NL-to-C\# generation achieves the highest runtime-pass rate (43\% mean) yet produces structurally vacuous scenes (mechanism $F_1 \approx 0.12$). Structural IR conditioning halves the runtime rate but recovers domain-faithful structure ($F_1$ up to 1.00). Within IR conditioning, behavior-only and full-scene granularity are statistically indistinguishable (McNemar $p = 1.0$), indicating input-level granularity saturation. These results show that compile rate is anti-correlated with functional correctness in this domain and that multi-axis evaluation is necessary to detect the divergence. We release the benchmark, replay logs, and per-record metrics for independent verification.

</details>


### [301] [MISA: Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07363)
*Ruijie Zhou,Fanxu Meng,Yufei Xu,Tongxuan Liu,Guangming Lu,Muhan Zhang,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MISA (Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention) 提出了一种轻量级的路由机制，将DSA中多个索引器头视为专家混合池，通过基于块级统计的路由器选择少量活跃头执行token级评分，在几乎不损失精度的情况下大幅降低计算成本，并提供分层变体进一步逼近原始DSA的选择结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA虽然通过学习式索引器实现了细粒度的推理时稀疏注意力，但其索引器使用了大量查询头（如DeepSeek-V3.2中的64头）且共享同一选定token集合，导致在长上下文场景下索引器的计算成本成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 将DSA的索引器头视为专家混合池，设计轻量级路由器利用廉价块级统计信息为每个查询选择少量活跃头，仅这些头执行繁重的token级评分；进一步提出分层变体，先通过路由过程保留扩展候选集，再使用原始DSA索引器进行重排序以精确恢复最终选定token。

Result: 仅用8个活跃头且无需额外训练，MISA在LongBench上匹配DSA索引器性能，索引器头数在DeepSeek-V3.2上减少8倍、GLM-5上减少4倍，平均优于HISA；在128K上下文下保持完全绿色的Needle-in-a-Haystack热力图，每层恢复超过92%的DSA索引器选定token；TileLang内核在单张NVIDIA H200 GPU上相比DSA原始索引器内核实现约3.82倍加速。

Conclusion: MISA通过混合专家路由策略有效解决了DSA索引器的计算瓶颈，在不牺牲精度的情况下显著提升了推理效率，并且无需额外训练即可直接替换现有DSA索引器。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) sets the state of the art for fine-grained inference-time sparse attention by introducing a learned token-wise indexer that scores every prefix token and selects the most relevant ones for the main attention. To remain expressive, the indexer uses many query heads (for example, 64 on DeepSeek-V3.2) that share the same selected token set; this multi-head design is precisely what makes the indexer the dominant cost on long contexts. We propose MISA (Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention), a drop-in replacement for the DSA indexer that treats its indexer heads as a pool of mixture-of-experts. A lightweight router uses cheap block-level statistics to pick a query-dependent subset of only a few active heads, and only those heads run the heavy token-level scoring. This preserves the diversity of the original indexer pool while reducing the per-query cost from scoring every prefix token with every head to scoring it with only a handful of routed heads, plus a negligible router term computed on a small set of pooled keys. We further introduce a hierarchical variant of MISA that uses the routed pass to keep an enlarged candidate set and then re-ranks it with the original DSA indexer to recover the final selected tokens almost exactly. With only eight active heads and no additional training, MISA matches the dense DSA indexer on LongBench across DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5 while running with eight and four times fewer indexer heads respectively, and outperforms HISA on average. It also preserves fully green Needle-in-a-Haystack heatmaps up to a 128K-token context and recovers more than 92% of the tokens selected by the DSA indexer per layer. Our TileLang kernel delivers roughly a 3.82 times speedup over DSA's original indexer kernel on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU.

</details>


### [302] [StreamPhy: Streaming Inference of High-Dimensional Physical Dynamics via State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07384)
*Panqi Chen,Yifan Sun,Shikai Fang,Xiao Fu,Lei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamPhy是一个端到端框架，通过集成自适应观测编码器、结构化状态空间模型和FT-FiLM解码器，实现对高维多模态物理场的高效流式推断，在精度上提升至少48%，推理速度比扩散模型快20-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏不规则测量数据中实时推断高维多模态物理场的演化是科学与工程领域的基础挑战，现有方法（扩散模型、函数张量方法）通常只能在离线设置下运行，依赖完整时间观测或推理成本高昂。

Method: StreamPhy包含三个核心组件：1) 数据自适应观测编码器，对任意观测模式具有鲁棒性；2) 结构化状态空间模型，支持不规则时间间隔上的内存高效在线更新；3) FT-FiLM（Functional Tensor Feature-wise Linear Modulation）解码器，用于连续场生成。理论上证明了FT-FiLM比函数Tucker模型更具表达能力。

Result: 在三个代表性物理系统的挑战性采样模式下，StreamPhy在精度上比最先进的基线方法提升至少48%，推理速度比扩散模型快20-100倍。

Conclusion: StreamPhy实现了高效准确的流式全物理场推断，在精度和速度上均显著优于现有方法，为实时物理场推断提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring the evolution of high-dimensional and multi-modal (e.g., spatio-temporal) physical fields from irregular sparse measurements in real time is a fundamental challenge in science and engineering. Existing approaches, including diffusion-based generative models and functional tensor methods, typically operate in offline settings, depend on full temporal observations, or incur substantial inference cost. We propose StreamPhy, an end-to-end framework that enables efficient and accurate streaming inference of full-field physical dynamics from incoming irregular sparse measurements. The framework integrates a data-adaptive observation encoder that is robust to arbitrary observation patterns, a structured state-space model that supports memory-efficient online updates across irregular time intervals, and an expressive Functional Tensor Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FT-FiLM) decoder for continuous-field generation. We prove that FT-FiLM is more expressive than the functional Tucker model, admitting a richer function class for handling complex dynamics. Experiments on three representative physical systems under challenging sampling patterns show that StreamPhy consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with at least 48\% improvement in accuracy and up to 20--100X faster inference than diffusion-based methods.

</details>


### [303] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07386)
*Karthick Krishna M.,Haricharan Balasundaram,Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CONES问题（嵌套演化可行集上的凸优化），设计了Lazy算法（实现O(T^{1-β})遗憾与O(T^β)移动成本的折中）和Frugal算法（对强凸/α-sharp损失函数实现零遗憾和O(log T)移动成本），并证明了后者在移动成本上的最优性（Ω(log T)下界）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的嵌套凸体追逐问题推广到更一般的优化框架CONES，旨在同时优化在线算法的遗憾（regret）和移动成本（movement cost），并探索不同凸性条件下两者之间的权衡关系。

Method: 针对一般凸损失函数提出Lazy算法，通过参数β调控遗憾与移动成本的折中；针对强凸或α-sharp损失函数提出Frugal算法，利用更积极的优化策略实现零遗憾与对数级的移动成本。

Result: Lazy算法实现了O(T^{1-β})遗憾和O(T^β)移动成本；Frugal算法实现了零遗憾和O(log T)移动成本；同时证明了任何在线算法在o(T)遗憾下移动成本的下界为Ω(log T)，表明Frugal是最优的。

Conclusion: CONES框架统一并推广了嵌套凸体追逐问题，所提算法在遗憾与移动成本之间实现了最优或接近最优的权衡，特别是Frugal算法在强凸/α-sharp条件下达到了帕累托最优。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} is considered where the objective function $f$ remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence $S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$. The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known nested convex body chasing problem. When the loss function is convex, we propose a lazy-algorithm and show that it achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost for any $β\in (0,1]$, over a time horizon of $T$. When the loss function is strongly convex or $α$-sharp, we propose an algorithm Frugal that simultaneously achieves zero regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with $o(T)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω(\log{T})$ for both cases, proving optimality of Frugal.

</details>


### [304] [Rubric-based On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07396)
*Junfeng Fang,Zhepei Hong,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Gengsheng Li,Houcheng Jiang,Dan Zhang,Haiyun Guo,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROPD框架，用结构化语义规则（rubrics）替代教师logits，实现黑盒场景下的同策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation），在多数场景下性能超越基于logits的先进方法，样本效率提升达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同策略蒸馏依赖教师模型的logits，这限制了其在黑盒场景（如仅能获取教师生成文本）中的应用。作者认为结构化语义规则可以作为教师logits的可扩展替代方案，从而实现在仅使用教师生成响应的情况下进行同策略优化。

Method: ROPD框架包含两个核心步骤：首先，通过教师-学生对比诱导出提示特定的结构化语义规则（rubrics）；然后，利用这些规则对学生模型的rollout进行评分，进而用于同策略优化（on-policy optimization）。

Result: 在大多数实验场景下，ROPD超越了先进的基于logits的同策略蒸馏方法，并且样本效率最高提升10倍。

Conclusion: 基于规则（rubric-based）的同策略蒸馏可以成为基于logits方法的灵活、兼容黑盒的替代方案，为跨专有和开源大语言模型的可扩展蒸馏提供了一个简单而强大的基线。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a powerful paradigm for model alignment, yet its reliance on teacher logits restricts its application to white-box scenarios. We contend that structured semantic rubrics can serve as a scalable alternative to teacher logits, enabling OPD using only teacher-generated responses. To prove it, we introduce ROPD, a simple yet foundational framework for rubric-based OPD. Specifically, ROPD induces prompt-specific rubrics from teacher-student contrasts, and then utilizes these rubrics to score the student rollouts for on-policy optimization. Empirically, ROPD outperforms the advanced logit-based OPD methods across most scenarios, and achieving up to a 10x gain in sample efficiency. These results position rubric-based OPD as a flexible, black-box-compatible alternative to the prevailing logit-based OPD, offering a simple yet strong baseline for scalable distillation across proprietary and open-source LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Peregrine123/ROPD_official.

</details>


### [305] [Have Graph -- Will Lift? The Case for Higher-Order Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07397)
*Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点论文，指出几何与拓扑在机器学习中已站稳脚跟，但模型多样性与可用基准数据集稀缺之间存在巨大矛盾，鼓励社区在提升现有图数据集之外，也积极寻找和构建新的数据集以支撑该研究领域的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何深度学习与拓扑深度学习领域模型发展迅速，但缺乏合适的基准数据集，研究者往往只能将现有图数据集提升（lifting）为高阶结构。作者希望鼓励社区走出这一局限，主动收集和创建新数据集，为领域提供更坚实的基础。

Method: 本文为观点论文（opinion paper），通过分析现状、指出问题并提出倡议的方式来论述。作者没有提出新的算法或模型，而是聚焦于数据集的缺失这一方法论层面的瓶颈。

Result: 指出当前领域存在“模型多样性”与“基准数据集稀缺”之间的显著矛盾，并呼吁社区在提升现有数据集之外，也主动发掘和发布新的真实世界数据集。

Conclusion: 为了推动拓扑深度学习领域的健康发展，社区需要更多高质量、真实世界的高阶结构数据集，而不仅仅依赖于对现有图数据集的提升（lifting）。

Abstract: After a somewhat rocky start, geometry and topology have established a foothold in machine learning. Message passing, either on graphs or higher-order complexes, is one of the main drivers of geometric deep learning, and paradigms that were once considered to be firmly in the realm of the abstract-like sheaves-have been "tamed" to serve as novel inductive biases for model architectures in topological deep learning. The veritable diversity of models, however, is in stark contrast to the scarcity of suitable benchmark datasets. As a result, researchers often resort to lifting existing graph datasets to include higher-order information. In this opinion paper, I want to encourage the community to also source new datasets, which may be used to prop up the foundations of our research field.

</details>


### [306] [Emergent Symbolic Structure in Health Foundation Models: Extraction, Alignment, and Cross-Modal Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07407)
*Gajendra Katuwal,Advait Koparkar,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Sarvesh Kirthivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种后训练框架，将健康基础模型的冻结嵌入分解为可解释的符号方向，实现无需重训练的跨模态对齐，发现不同传感器模态间共享可解释的符号组织，跨模态迁移保留95%以上性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康基础模型从可穿戴传感器学习有用表征，但解释其编码内容以及训练后在模态间迁移知识仍然困难。

Method: 提出后训练框架，将冻结嵌入分解为可解释的符号方向，利用这些符号对齐不同模态的嵌入空间，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 提取的符号与健康状况和生理属性选择性关联，且部分关联在模态和架构间共享；跨模态迁移保留95%以上域内性能，近似对称，有限配对数据下即饱和，表明对齐恢复了共享的低维生理信息子空间。

Conclusion: 健康基础模型嵌入包含可解释的符号组织，该组织在模态间共享，无需联合训练即可支持跨域迁移。

Abstract: Health foundation models (FMs) learn useful representations from wearable sensors, but interpreting what they encode and transferring that knowledge across modalities after training remains difficult. We present a post-training framework that decomposes frozen embeddings into interpretable directions, referred to as symbols, and use these symbols to align the embedding spaces without retraining. We evaluate the framework on three FMs for photoplethysmography (PPG) and accelerometer data, independently pretrained on ~20M minutes of unlabeled data from ~172K participants, and analyzed on a held-out cohort of 30K subjects. We find that extracted symbols associate selectively with health conditions and physiological attributes, and these associations are partially shared across modalities and architectures. Cross-modal transfer via symbols retains more than 95% of in-domain performance, is nearly symmetric across domain directions, and saturates with limited paired data, together indicating that alignment recovers a shared low-dimensional subspace rich in physiological information. Overall, these results suggest that health FM embeddings contain an interpretable symbolic organization that is shared across modalities and supports cross-domain transfer without joint training.

</details>


### [307] [Tracking Large-scale Shared Bikes with Inertial Motion Learning in GNSS Blocked Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07412)
*Feng Liu,Kejia Li,Zhiwei Yang,Chunwei Yang,Qun Li,Guobin Wu,Qiang Ni,Ruipeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合自行车机械约束与混合专家模型的惯性跟踪框架，利用踏板与后轮间的机械传动关系将骑行者的周期性蹬踏行为转化为车轮速度进行动态校准，在滴滴共享单车真实数据上相比基线方法提升至少12%的定位精度，车轮速度95分位误差低于0.5 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS在城郊峡谷等复杂骑行环境中失效，而仅使用低成本惯性传感器的定位存在累积漂移和滤波方法鲁棒性差的问题；视觉和LiDAR虽能提供可靠测量但不适合大规模部署。

Method: 提出一种惯性跟踪框架，融合自行车机械约束与混合专家模型：利用多个专家模块捕获共享表示并通过门控机制加权，实现多任务学习和不确定性感知轨迹估计；基于踏板与后轮间的机械传动关系，将骑行者的周期性蹬踏行为与加速度变化的内在规律转换为车轮速度用于动态校准。

Result: 在滴滴共享单车真实骑行数据上，系统相比基线方法定位精度提升至少12%，车轮速度95分位误差低于0.5 m/s。

Conclusion: 融合自行车机械约束与混合专家模型的惯性跟踪框架能有效提升低成本传感器在复杂骑行环境下的定位精度和鲁棒性，适合大规模部署。

Abstract: Although Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) provide a general solution for bike tracking outdoors, there still exist complex riding environments where only inertial navigation systems work, such as urban canyons. Despite decades of research, localization using only low-cost inertial sensors still faces challenges such as cumulative drifts and poor robustness caused by filtering methods. Furthermore, sensors such as visual and LiDAR could provide reliable measurements, but they are not suitable for large-scale deployment. In this paper, we propose an inertial tracking framework that integrates bicycle mechanical constraints with a mixture-of-experts model. Specifically, we leverage multiple expert modules to capture shared representations and weight them through the gating mechanism, thus improving multi-task learning performance and enabling uncertainty-aware trajectory estimation. Furthermore, based on the mechanical transmission between the pedal and the rear wheel of a bike, we explore the intrinsic relationship between the rider's periodic pedalling behaviors and acceleration variations, and convert such patterns into bike's wheel speed for dynamic calibration. Experiments with real-world riding data from shared bikes of the DiDi ride-hailing platform demonstrate that our system improves the accuracy of baselines by at least 12%, with wheel speed errors below 0.5 m/s at 95-percentile.

</details>


### [308] [Risk-Consistent Multiclass Learning from Random Label-Subset Membership Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07413)
*Jiaxu Su,Junpeng Li,Changchun Hua,Yana Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在随机标签子集查询下的多类弱监督学习框架，通过建模查询-响应数据生成分布，推导目标风险的无偏估计器，并提出非负和绝对值修正的风险估计器以解决负经验风险和过拟合问题，理论分析给出了泛化界和一致性结果，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取精确类别标签成本高、不可靠或受隐私等条件限制，而询问标注者真实标签是否属于某个标签子集则更容易实现。现有弱监督学习缺乏对这种直接通过查询-响应观测生成弱监督信息的系统性刻画。

Method: 将随机标签子集查询下的弱监督学习形式化为一个多类学习框架；建模查询-响应的数据生成分布；在经验风险最小化(ERM)框架下推导目标风险的无偏估计器；针对负经验风险和过拟合问题，引入非负修正(non-negative)和绝对值修正(absolute-value)两种修正风险估计器。

Result: 无偏估计器具有条件泛化界和过量风险界；修正风险估计器具有偏差和一致性结果；在匹配随机查询机制下的实验验证了直接查询-响应学习的可行性以及风险修正的稳定化效果。

Conclusion: 通过标签子集查询获取弱监督信息是一种可行的学习范式，所提出的风险修正方法能有效稳定学习过程，为实际中低成本获取监督信息提供了理论支撑和方法保证。

Abstract: Obtaining accurate class labels is often costly or unreliable, and may also be limited by privacy or other practical conditions. Compared with asking an annotator to provide the exact class, it is often easier to ask whether the true label belongs to a certain label subset. This query-response form defines a distinct weak-supervision mechanism: weak supervision information is generated through feedback on a label subset. Although weakly supervised learning has studied many learning frameworks, most existing work starts from established weak label objects. A systematic characterization is still lacking for weakly supervised learning generated directly by such query response observations. This paper proposes a multiclass learn ing framework under random label-subset queries. We model the data-generating distribution of query-response observations and derive an unbiased estimator of the target risk under the empirical risk minimization (ERM) framework. To address negative empirical risk and the associated overfitting problem, we introduce corrected risk estimators based on non-negative and absolute-value corrections. Theoretical analysis establishes a conditional generalization and excess-risk bound for the unbiased estimator, and a bias-and-consistency result for the corrected risk estimator. Experiments under the matched random-query mechanism demonstrate the feasibility of direct query-response learning and the stabilization effect of risk correction.

</details>


### [309] [SR$^2$-LoRA: Self-Rectifying Inter-layer Relations in Low-Rank Adaptation for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07420)
*Fengqiang Wan,Yipeng Lin,Kan Lv,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SR²-LoRA方法，通过约束层间关系漂移（inter-layer relation drift）来缓解基于参数高效微调的类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘。该方法构造新旧模型在当前任务样本上的关系矩阵并对其奇异值进行对齐，理论证明该对齐方式比逐元素对齐更具鲁棒性。实验表明该方法有效缓解遗忘，且随任务数增加优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在类增量学习（CIL）中仍存在灾难性遗忘问题。作者从层间关系漂移的新视角分析灾难性遗忘，发现学习新任务时层间表征关系的逐渐破坏会压缩先前任务的分类边界，从而降低模型性能。

Method: 提出SR²-LoRA（Self-Rectifying inter-layer Relation Low-Rank Adaptation），具体方法为：构建当前模型和先前模型在当前任务样本上的层间关系矩阵，并对它们的奇异值进行对齐，从而约束层间关系漂移。该方法理论上比直接逐元素对齐具有更强的估计扰动鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准CIL基准测试上的大量实验表明，SR²-LoRA有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，且随着任务数量的增加，其优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 通过约束层间关系漂移可以有效缓解类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘，奇异值对齐策略比逐元素对齐更具鲁棒性，为PEFT-based CIL提供了新思路。

Abstract: Pre-trained models with parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) have demonstrated promising potential for class-incremental learning (CIL), yet catastrophic forgetting still persists when adapting models to new tasks. In this paper, we present a novel perspective on catastrophic forgetting through the analysis of inter-layer relation drift, i.e., the progressive disruption of relationships among layer-wise representations during the learning of new tasks. We theoretically show that the increase of such drift reduces the classification margins of previously learned tasks, thereby degrading overall model performance. To address this issue, we propose \underline{S}elf-\underline{R}ectifying inter-layer \underline{R}elation Low-Rank Adaptation~(SR$^2$-LoRA), a simple yet effective method that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by constraining inter-layer relation drift. Specifically, SR$^2$-LoRA constructs the relation matrices induced by the previous and current models on current-task samples, and aligns the corresponding singular values. We further theoretically show that this alignment exhibits greater robustness to estimation perturbations than direct entry-wise alignment. Extensive experiments on standard CIL benchmarks demonstrate that SR$^2$-LoRA effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting, with its advantages becoming more pronounced as the number of tasks increases. Code is available in the \href{https://github.com/FqWan24/SR-2-LoRA}{repository}.

</details>


### [310] [A Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering Algorithm for Archive-Scale Online Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07424)
*Shao Shi,Xin Yang,Huiran Feng,Jianhuai Ye,Tianlong Hu,Yaling Zeng,Tzung-May Fu,Lei Zhu,Huizhong Shen,Chen Wang,Shu Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FASC（灵活自适应稳定聚类）框架，通过解耦相似性核与优化逻辑，结合密度增强相似性选择规则和几何约束，实现了大规模质谱数据的高效、确定性和线性复杂度聚类。在2500万大气气溶胶质谱数据上验证，FASC展现了严格的线性运行时间（O(N)），成功自主映射二次无机气溶胶的大气老化路径，并分离出丰度低于0.2%的超稀有工业示踪物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法在可扩展性、度量灵活性和算法稳定性三者之间存在不可调和的权衡，成为从多TB级质谱数据流中提取化学信息的计算瓶颈。

Method: FASC采用动力学系统框架，架构上解耦相似性核与优化逻辑；引入密度增强相似性选择（Density-Augmented Similarity Selection）规则和几何约束，保证确定性、顺序无关的收敛。

Result: 在标准机器学习基准上达到>99.5%的簇纯度和0.99的调整兰德指数；在2500万质谱数据上展示严格线性运行时间（O(N)）；成功识别大气老化路径和超稀有工业示踪物（<0.2%丰度）。

Conclusion: FASC为大规模环境大数据挖掘提供了可扩展的基础设施，克服了传统启发式方法存在的随机漂移和算法混合问题。

Abstract: Modern online mass spectrometry generates multi-terabyte data streams critical for understanding Earth's environmental systems. However, extracting actionable chemical insights from these repositories is impeded by a computational bottleneck: existing clustering methods force a compromise among scalability, metric flexibility, and algorithmic stability. Here, we introduce Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering (FASC), a dynamical systems framework that resolves these constraints by architecturally decoupling the similarity kernel from rigorous optimization logic. Unlike legacy heuristics that suffer from stochastic drift and algorithmic blending, FASC employs a Density-Augmented Similarity Selection rule and geometric constraints to guarantee deterministic, order-independent convergence. After validating FASC on canonical machine-learning ground truths (achieving >99.5% cluster purity and 0.99 Adjusted Rand Index), we deployed the framework on 25 million mass spectra of atmospheric aerosols. Demonstrating strictly linear empirical runtime scaling (O(N)), FASC autonomously mapped atmospheric aging pathways of secondary inorganic aerosols while isolating ultra-rare industrial tracers (<0.2% abundance), providing a scalable infrastructure for mining environmental big data.

</details>


### [311] [GameGen-Verifier: Parallel Keypoint-Based Verification for LLM-Generated Games via Runtime State Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07442)
*Chaobo Jia,Ruipeng Wan,Ting Sun,Weihao Tan,Borui Wan,Yuxuan Tong,Guangming Sheng,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM生成游戏的自动化验证难题，提出GameGen-Verifier范式，将规范分解为可验证关键点并转化为独立验证单元，在VeriGame数据集上达到92.2%准确率（基线58.8%），耗时降低16.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM游戏生成缺乏可靠的自动化验证方法，Agent-as-a-Verifier方法将验证混入开放性游戏过程中，存在结果不可达、耗时长、覆盖有限且受限于智能体游戏能力等问题。

Method: 将游戏规范分解为可验证关键点，每个关键点转化为独立验证单元：将游戏运行时打补丁到具体目标状态，执行有界交互，然后根据关键点断言判断结果。实现了GGV-Harness可扩展智能体框架，提供并发管理、运行时隔离和故障恢复。

Result: 在涵盖7种游戏类型的100个游戏的VeriGame数据集上，GameGen-Verifier达到92.2%的准确率（基线58.8%），同时将耗时降低最多16.6倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的分解式验证范式显著优于覆盖增强的Agent-as-a-Verifier基线方法，验证效率和准确性均有大幅提升。

Abstract: LLM-based game generation promises to turn natural-language specifications into executable games, but progress is limited by the lack of reliable automated verification. Unlike conventional code generation, game correctness is defined over long-horizon interaction: a game may appear correct while violating core mechanics such as state updates, interaction rules, and phase transitions. Existing Agent-as-a-Verifier approaches collapse verification into open-ended gameplay, making verdicts reachability-bound, time-consuming, coverage-limited, and sensitive to the agent's gameplay ability.
  We present GameGen-Verifier, an automated verification paradigm for LLM-generated games that decomposes a specification into verifiable keypoints and grounds them into independent verification units. Each unit patches the game runtime into a concrete target state, executes a bounded interaction, and judges the outcome against the keypoint assertion. We implement GGV-Harness, a scalable agentic harness providing concurrency management, runtime isolation, and fault recovery.
  On VeriGame, our dataset of 100 games across seven genres, GameGen-Verifier achieves up to 92.2% accuracy against human judgments versus 58.8% for the coverage-enforced Agent-as-a-Verifier baseline, while reducing wall-clock time by up to 16.6x.

</details>


### [312] [Inference-Time Attribute Distribution Alignment for Unconditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07456)
*Hao Luan,See-Kiong Ng,Chun Kai Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优控制的推理时属性分布对齐方法，通过向逆扩散过程添加时变扰动，在不重新训练或微调预训练无条件扩散模型的情况下，实现生成样本群体在属性分布上的可控对齐（如人口统计平衡或语义比例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时可控生成技术主要关注单个样本的控制，无法满足需要整个样本群体遵循特定属性分布（如人口统计平衡或语义比例）的实际应用需求。

Method: 将推理时属性分布对齐形式化为预训练无条件扩散模型的最优控制问题：将逆扩散过程视为动力系统的滚落，通过添加时变扰动作为控制信号，使用基于最优控制的算法优化可微的分布匹配目标，同时惩罚控制代价以保持数据保真度。

Result: 在图像生成实验中，所提出的即插即用方法在将属性分布对齐到多样且灵活的测试时目标方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法无需重新训练或微调预训练模型，即可有效实现推理时属性分布对齐，具有即插即用特性。

Abstract: Inference-time controllable generation is essential for real-world applications of unconditional diffusion models. However, most existing techniques focus on individual samples, struggling in applications that require the sample population to follow specific attribute distributions (e.g., demographic balance or semantic proportions). We formalize this setting as the inference-time attribute distributional alignment problem for pretrained unconditional diffusion models. To address this, we cast inference-time attribute distributional alignment as an optimal control problem over the reverse diffusion process, viewing the process as the rollout of a dynamical system and augmenting it with additive, time-dependent perturbations as control. We solve for the perturbations using an optimal-control-based algorithm to optimize a differentiable distribution-matching objective while penalizing control effort to preserve data fidelity. Experiment results in image generation demonstrate that our proposed plug-and-play approach can better align attribute distributions to diverse and flexible test-time targets compared to baselines, without retraining or finetuning the pretrained diffusion model.

</details>


### [313] [Estimation of Motor Unit Parameters from Surface Electromyograms using an Informed Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07458)
*Kaja Balzereit,Malte Mechtenberg,Axel Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息自动编码器的方法，能够从表面肌电信号中同时非侵入性估计多个运动单元参数（如神经支配区中心和传导速度），在合成数据上实现了高精度估计（神经支配区中心MAE=2.6mm，传导速度MAE=0.17m/s），减少了手动建模工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动单元参数（如神经支配区中心、电位传导速度）对提高神经力学模型的保真度至关重要，但现有非侵入性估计方法主要依赖白盒建模，需要大量手动建模工作，且难以同时估计多个参数。

Method: 开发了一种信息自动编码器，在潜空间中学习参数的同时重建表面EMG记录，并遵循将参数与EMG信号关联起来的物理定律，从而将非线性逆问题转化为可学习的框架。

Result: 在合成数据实验中，神经支配区中心估计的MAE为2.5989 mm，电位传导速度估计的MAE为0.1697 m/s。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了同时估计多个运动单元参数的可行性，通过数据驱动的机器学习减少了手动建模工作量，为神经力学模型提供了一种新的非侵入性参数估计途径。

Abstract: Motor unit parameters such as the innervation zone centre or the conduction velocity of the electrical potential harbour the potential to improve the fidelity of neuromechanical models used for movement and force prediction. Determining these parameters in a non-invasive way is challenging, as they are subject-specific and may vary with muscle contraction. Existing work on the estimation of motor unit parameters mainly relies on white-box modelling and therefore requires substantial manual modelling effort. This work targets the simultaneous estimation of multiple subject-specific motor unit parameters from electromyography (EMG) recordings measured non-invasively at the skin surface. This results in an inverse problem with a nonlinear loss function. To address this problem, an informed autoencoder is developed. This autoencoder reconstructs the surface EMG recordings while learning the parameters in its latent space and adhering to physical laws that relate the parameters to the EMG signals. In experiments on synthetic data, innervation zone centres are estimated with a mean absolute error of 2.5989 $\mathrm{mm}$, and conduction velocities of the electric potential are estimated with a mean absolute error of 0.1697 $\mathrm{m}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$. These results demonstrate the plausibility of this novel approach, which enables the simultaneous estimation of several motor unit parameters while reducing manual modelling effort through the integration of data-driven machine learning.

</details>


### [314] [Approximation Error Upper and Lower Bounds for Hölder Class with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07463)
*Xin He,Yuling Jiao,Xiliang Lu,Jerry Zhijian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer在Hölder函数类上的表达能力，给出了近似误差的上下界：上界证明需$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-d_0/\alpha})$个模块，下界证明至少需$\Omega(\varepsilon^{-d_0/(4\alpha)})$个模块，并扩展至回归任务得到过剩风险率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准Transformer架构（含Softmax、ReLU和残差连接）在Hölder函数类上的表达能力，填补理论近似上下界的空白。

Method: 理论分析：推导近似误差上界；利用VC维上界证明近似误差下界；将结论推广至一般回归任务并分析过剩风险率。

Result: 上界：$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-d_0/\alpha})$个模块可达到$\varepsilon$精度；下界：至少需要$\Omega(\varepsilon^{-d_0/(4\alpha)})$个模块。

Conclusion: 为Transformer的近似能力提供了首个严格的理论上下界，并从理论层面支持了其在回归任务中的实证有效性。

Abstract: We explore the expressive power of Transformers by establishing precise approximation error upper and lower bounds for Hölder class. Specifically, a new approximation upper bound is derived for the standard Transformer architecture equipped with Softmax operators, ReLU activation functions, and residual connections. We prove that a Transformer network composed of at most $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-{d_{0}}/α})$ blocks can approximate any bounded Hölder function with $d_{0}$-dimensional input and smoothness $α\in(0,1]$ under any accuracy $\varepsilon>0$. In the case of approximation lower bounds, leveraging the VC-dimension upper bound, we are the first to rigorously prove that Transformers demand for at least $Ω(\varepsilon^{-{d_{0}}/({4α})})$ blocks to achieve the $\varepsilon$ approximation accuracy. As a final step, we extend the derived results for standard Transformers to a general regression task and establish the corresponding excess risk rates demonstrating Transformers' empirical effectiveness in real-world settings.

</details>


### [315] [Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation for Fast Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07661)
*George Rapakoulias,Peter Garud,Lingjiong Zhu,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STMD是一种无教师框架，通过蒸馏随机微分方程的转移映射来加速扩散模型推理，实现一步或几步随机采样，同时保留概率生成特性，在多个图像数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然生成质量高，但推理成本昂贵。现有蒸馏方法通常需要预训练教师模型、双层优化或轨迹模拟缓存，训练效率低且复杂。

Method: 提出STMD框架，蒸馏采样SDE的完整转移映射而非仅去噪均值；用条件均值流模型参数化SDE转移，得到一步或几步随机采样器；无需预训练教师、双层优化或轨迹模拟缓存。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CelebA数据集上验证了图像生成效果；推导了Wasserstein距离下的收敛界，提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: STMD是一种高效可扩展的扩散模型蒸馏方法，在保持随机采样能力的同时显著加速推理，适用于扩散后验采样、逆问题、能量基微调等需要随机推理的下游任务。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong generation quality, diversity, and distribution coverage, but their performance often comes with expensive inference. In this work, we propose Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation (STMD), a teacher-free framework for accelerating diffusion model inference while preserving probabilistic sample generation. In contrast to score-based diffusion models, whose denoising parametrization models the mean of the posterior distribution, STMD distills the full transition map associated with the sampling stochastic differential equation (SDE). We parameterize these SDE transitions with a conditional Mean Flow model, yielding a one- or few-step stochastic sampler that retains the transition structure of the underlying diffusion process. This perspective is especially useful for downstream tasks that require stochastic inference, such as diffusion posterior sampling, inverse problems, and energy-based fine-tuning. Compared to recent distillation methods, STMD requires no pretrained teacher, bi-level optimization, or trajectory simulation and caching, enabling efficient and scalable training. We derive convergence bounds for our method in the Wasserstein distance, providing a strong theoretical foundation for our approach, and validate STMD on various image generation examples on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CelebA datasets.

</details>


### [316] [Physical Simulators as Do-Operators: Causal Discovery under Latent Confounders for AI-for-Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07467)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CFM-SD方法，利用第一性原理物理模拟器作为do算子，在存在潜在混杂变量的情况下进行因果发现，仅需O(d)次单变量干预即可识别d变量因果结构，在合成数据和真实科学数据上均显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（IGSP、DCDI、ENCO）假设因果充分性（无潜在混杂变量）并依赖合成模拟器中的虚拟干预，但在AI-for-Science场景（如分子设计、材料科学）中，潜在混杂变量普遍存在，且真实干预（如基于物理的模拟）每次需数小时至数天，现有方法无法适用。

Method: 提出CFM-SD（Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data），将第一性原理物理模拟器作为Pearl干预演算中的do算子，结合因果流匹配框架，同时处理潜在混杂变量和真实干预数据。理论上证明了在物理可实现约束下，d变量因果结构可通过O(d)次单变量干预识别。

Result: 内在评估（合成数据，γ=0.2-0.8）：CFM-SD平均F1=0.800，基线方法F1=0.127-0.562。外在评估（真实科学数据）：分子毒性预测偏差降低57%，电池电解质优化偏差降低58%。

Conclusion: CFM-SD在存在潜在混杂变量的科学场景中实现了高效、实用的因果发现，显著优于现有方法，并展示了超越合成基准的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Existing interventional causal discovery methods -- IGSP, DCDI, ENCO -- assume causal sufficiency (no latent confounders) and rely on virtual interventions in synthetic simulators. In AI-for-Science settings such as molecular design and materials science, latent confounders are ubiquitous and real interventions (e.g., physics-based simulations) require hours to days per data point. We propose CFM-SD (Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data), which uses first-principles physical simulators as do-operators in Pearl's interventional calculus to simultaneously handle latent confounders and real interventional data. Theoretically, $d$-variable causal structure is identifiable with $O(d)$ single-variable interventions -- the minimum under physical realizability constraints. In Intrinsic Evaluation on synthetic data ($γ=0.2$--$0.8$), CFM-SD achieves average F1$=0.800$ vs. F1$=0.127$--$0.562$ for all baselines. In Extrinsic Evaluation on real scientific data, CFM-SD achieves 57--58\% bias reduction in molecular toxicity prediction and battery electrolyte optimization, demonstrating practical value beyond synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [317] [Enhancing Federated Quadruplet Learning: Stochastic Client Selection and Embedding Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07888)
*Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedQuad通过对比学习（最小化类内距离、最大化类间距离）来缓解联邦学习中数据异构性导致的表征对齐问题，在多个非IID数据集上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构性（特别是有限数据与类别不平衡）导致全局模型泛化性能下降，现有方法在缓解表征对齐问题上效果有限。

Method: 提出FedQuad方法，通过联合优化正样本对距离最小化与负样本对距离最大化，显式约束类内表征紧凑性与类间可分性，以减轻模型聚合过程中的表征错位。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上，多种非IID设置及不同客户端数量下，FedQuad均持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 度量学习在集中式和联邦环境下均能有效缓解异构数据分布下的表征坍塌问题，FedQuad是对该方向的成功实践。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables decentralised model training across distributed clients without requiring data centralisation. However, the generalisation performance of the global model is usually degraded by data heterogeneity across clients, particularly under limited data availability and class imbalance. To address this challenge, we propose FedQuad, a novel method that explicitly enforces minimising intra-class representations while enabling inter-class splits across clients. By jointly minimising distances between positive pairs and maximising distances between negative pairs, the proposed approach mitigates representation misalignment introduced during model aggregation. We evaluate our method on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under diverse non-IID settings and varying numbers of clients, demonstrating consistent improvements over existing baselines. Additionally, we provide a comprehensive analysis of metric learning-based approaches in both centralised and federated environments, highlighting their effectiveness in alleviating representation collapse under heterogeneous data distributions.

</details>


### [318] [Flatness and Gradient Alignment Are Both Necessary: Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration for Multi-Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07914)
*Aristotelis Ballas,Christos Diou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了多分布学习场景下的超额风险分解，发现平坦性与梯度对齐两个几何性质在结构上无法相互替代，并提出同时优化两者的SAGE方法，在领域泛化和多任务学习基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有锐度感知方法和梯度对齐方法各自只关注损失景观的单一几何性质（平坦性或梯度对齐），忽略了另一个性质。本文证明这种忽略在结构上不可避免，两者在多分布学习中应同时被考虑。

Method: 首先推导超额风险分解，得到两个主要项：(i) 对齐项，由 $\bar{H}^{-1}\Sigma_g$ 的迹控制；(ii) 曲率项，由 $\bar{H}$ 控制。提出SAGE方法：曲率组件用每层梯度矩阵的极因子替换SAM的梯度缩放扰动（通过Newton-Schulz迭代实现），使上升步探索各方向幅度相近；对齐组件在下行步注入各向同性噪声，噪声大小与跨分布梯度不一致性成比例。

Result: 在5个领域泛化基准（DomainBed）和2个多任务学习基准上，SAGE方法在DomainBed上取得新SOTA，作为通用改进方案优于或持平现有MTL求解器。

Conclusion: 平坦性和梯度对齐在多分布学习中均不可或缺，SAGE通过同时优化两者实现了更优的泛化性能，为多分布学习提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware and gradient-alignment methods have been shown to improve generalization, however each family of methods targets a single geometric property of the loss landscape, while ignoring the other. In this paper, we show that this omission is structurally unavoidable and that both flatness and gradient alignment should be considered in multi-distribution learning settings. Specifically, we derive an excess-risk decomposition that yields two additive leading-order terms: (i) an alignment term, controlled by the trace of $\bar{H}^{-1}Σ_g$ and (ii) a curvature term, controlled by $\bar{H}$, where $\bar{H}$ is the average Hessian and $Σ_g$ is the covariance of the gradient across distributions. Notably, $\bar{H}$ appears inverted in one and non-inverted in the other. We further show, via a counterexample, that neither quantity bounds the other in general, so no algorithm targeting only one term can guarantee low excess risk. Motivated by this decomposition, we propose SAGE (Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration) that targets both terms. The curvature component replaces SAM's gradient-scaled perturbation with the polar factor of each layer's gradient matrix, computed via Newton-Schulz iteration, so that the ascent step probes all directions with similar magnitude. On the other hand, the alignment component injects isotropic noise at the descent step, the magnitude of which scales with cross-distribution gradient disagreement. Experiments on five domain-generalization and two multi-task learning benchmarks show that the proposed method establishes a new state-of-the-art on DomainBed and acts as a general-purpose improvement to base MTL solvers, remaining competitive with, or even surpassing, state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [319] [NPMixer: Hierarchical Neighboring Patch Mixing for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07476)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NPMixer，一种层次化架构，通过可学习平稳小波变换自适应分解信号，利用邻域混合器块捕获局部时序动态，并通过通道混合编码器学习高频成分的通道相关性，在7个数据集上28个实验中的20个（71.4%）取得最优MSE结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测面临局部时序动态与全局变量依赖复杂性的挑战，现有方法难以同时捕获多尺度时序模式与通道间依赖关系。

Method: 提出NPMixer层次架构：1) 可学习平稳小波变换，数据自适应地学习滤波器系数，将信号分解为趋势和细节分量；2) 邻域混合器块，通过层级MLP层操作非重叠patch捕获局部时序动态，扩展感受野以捕获多尺度依赖；3) 通道混合编码器，应用于高频分量学习通道相关性，同时保持全局趋势稳定性。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上，NPMixer在28个实验设置中的20个（71.4%）取得MSE最优性能，一致性地超越当前最先进模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的层次化邻域混合架构结合可学习小波变换，能有效同时建模局部时序动态和全局通道依赖，显著提升多变量时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting remains a challenge due to the complexity of local temporal dynamics and global dependencies across multiple variables.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{N}eighboring \textbf{P}atching \textbf{Mixer} (\textbf{NPMixer}), a hierarchical architecture featuring a Learnable Stationary Wavelet Transform that adaptively learns filter coefficients to decompose signals into trend and detail components in a data-dependent manner.
  Our framework introduces a Neighboring Mixer Block that captures local temporal dynamics through a series of hierarchical MLP layers operating on non-overlapping patches.
  Specifically, the mixer block utilizes MLPs to learn temporal patterns within and across these patches, expanding the receptive field to capture multi-scale dependencies.
  A Channel-Mixing Encoder is applied to high-frequency components to learn channel correlations while preserving the stability of the underlying global trend.
  Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that NPMixer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, achieving better performance in 20 out of 28 ($71.4\%$) evaluated experimental setups for MSE.

</details>


### [320] [SHRED: Retain-Set-Free Unlearning via Self-Distillation with Logit Demotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07482)
*Zizhao Hu,Ameya Godbole,Johnny Tian-Zheng Wei,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHRED方法，一种无需保留集的大语言模型机器遗忘技术，通过识别遗忘样本中高信息量（低概率）的token进行选择性遗忘，利用KL自蒸馏目标同时实现遗忘效果与模型效用保持，在四个基准上达到帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘方法大多依赖保留集来防止模型效用灾难性下降，这引入了额外的数据依赖并增加了部署复杂性。作者希望设计一种无需保留集且不牺牲遗忘效果的遗忘方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 选择阶段：对遗忘集实例做前向传播，收集逐token自回归概率，选择最低概率（最高信息量）的token作为遗忘位置，其余位置作为良性锚点；(2) 训练阶段：构建修改后的KL目标，在遗忘位置降低记忆token的logit，在良性位置保留原始分布，通过单一KL自蒸馏目标同时驱动遗忘和效用保持。

Result: 在四个标准遗忘基准上，SHRED建立了新的帕累托最优遗忘效果-模型效用权衡，超越依赖保留集的方法；对重新学习攻击和成员推断攻击具有鲁棒性；在多次顺序遗忘后仍能保持稳定的模型效用。

Conclusion: SHRED通过识别遗忘样本中真正携带记忆信息的高信息量token并选择性遗忘，成功消除了保留集依赖，同时实现了更优的遗忘-效用权衡，是一种实用且高效的LLM遗忘方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to selectively remove memorized content such as private data, copyrighted text, or hazardous knowledge, without costly full retraining. Most existing methods require a retain set of curated examples to prevent catastrophic degradation of general model utility, creating an extra data dependency that complicates deployment. We propose SHRED (Self-distillation via High-surprisal-only Retain-set-free Entropy Demotion), a retain-set-free unlearning method built on a key insight: not all tokens within a forget set instance carry memorized information equally. High-information tokens concentrate the model's memorized knowledge, while low-information tokens reflect general language competence. SHRED operates in two stages. (1) Selection: We perform a forward pass on a forget set instance, collect per-token autoregressive probabilities, and select the bottom (lowest probability, highest Shannon information) as forget positions; the remaining positions are retained as benign anchors. (2) Training: We construct modified KL targets that demote the memorized token's logit at forget positions while preserving the original distribution at benign positions. The model is then trained via a single top KL self-distillation objective that simultaneously drives forgetting and utility preservation. We evaluate SHRED across four standard unlearning benchmarks and demonstrate that it establishes a new Pareto-optimal trade-off between forget efficacy and model utility, outperforming retain-set-dependent methods. Our analysis shows that SHRED is robust against relearning attacks and membership-inference attacks, and it maintains stable utility even after many sequential unlearning runs.

</details>


### [321] [Excluding the Target Domain Improves Extrapolation: Deconfounded Hierarchical Physics Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07485)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出去混杂层级门控(DHG)机制，通过反事实估计和后门调整消除温度混杂效应，结合粗细粒度物理约束，在锂离子电池温度外推任务上实现RMSE 0.215（相比基线提升46%），并发现预训练时排除目标域数据可使外推性能提升39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理约束深度生成模型在处理分布外外推时存在两大问题：一是将物理约束视为单一静态正则项，未考虑物理定律的层级结构；二是未解决混杂变量问题（如温度混淆效应），导致模型捕捉到的是虚假相关性而非真正的物理不一致性。

Method: 提出去混杂层级门控(DHG)机制，包含三部分：1) 通过do算子进行反事实估计识别温度混杂何时以多强程度污染各约束层级；2) 采用后门调整(backdoor adjustment)移除混杂效应；3) 应用粗细粒度(Coarse-to-Fine)物理约束渐进式生成。

Result: 在锂离子电池温度外推基准（训练于24°C，评估于4.0-43.0°C）上，DHG方法RMSE=0.215，相比无约束基线Pure CFM（0.397）提升46%。预训练时排除目标域数据比包含目标域数据的外推性能提升39%（RMSE 0.224 vs 0.324）。

Conclusion: FNO在学习领域无关的物理模式时，排除目标域数据反而能更有效地迁移；DHG通过解耦混杂效应和层级物理约束，显著提升了物理约束生成模型在分布外条件下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Extrapolation to out-of-distribution conditions is a fundamental challenge for physics-constrained deep generative models. Existing methods apply physical constraints as a single static regularization term uniformly across the generation process, and address neither the hierarchical structure of physical laws and the confounding variable problem. We propose the Deconfounded Hierarchical Gate (DHG), which serves as a diagnostic and control mechanism: it identifies when and how strongly temperature confounding contaminates each constraint level, so that hierarchical gates reflect intrinsic physical inconsistency rather than spurious temperature effects. DHG combines counterfactual estimation via the do-operator with backdoor adjustment to remove confounding, then applies Coarse-to-Fine physical constraints progressively. We report a counter-intuitive finding in pretraining: excluding the target-domain data from pretraining outperforms including it by 39% in extrapolation performance (RMSE 0.224 vs. 0.324). This occurs because FNO learns domain-agnostic physical patterns that transfer more effectively when the target domain is withheld. On a lithium-ion battery temperature extrapolation benchmark (trained at 24 degrees Celsius, evaluated at 4.0--43.0 degrees Celsius), our method achieves RMSE = 0.215, a 46% improvement over the unconstrained baseline (Pure CFM: 0.397).

</details>


### [322] [SGD for Variational Inference: Tackling Unbounded Variance via Preconditioning and Dynamic Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07531)
*Hippolyte Labarrière,Cesare Molinari,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了黑盒变分推断（BBVI）中随机梯度方差无界（满足Blum-Gladyshev条件）时的优化理论，证明了椭圆位置-尺度分布族中ELBO解的存在性，并为配备动态批处理和预条件的小批量投影SGD提供了有限时间和渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBVI中随机梯度的方差无界，违反标准SGD假设，仅满足较弱的BG条件（方差随距离最优点的距离二次增长），理论和实践之间存在缺口，需要弥合。

Method: 聚焦椭圆位置-尺度分布族；证明ELBO解的存在性；采用小批量投影SGD（PSGD），结合动态批处理和预条件技术；在BG条件下建立有限时间和渐近收敛保证。

Result: 1) 证明了椭圆位置-尺度分布族中ELBO解的存在性；2) 在BG条件下，为配备动态批处理和预条件的PSGD建立了全面的收敛保证（有限时间和渐近）；3) 数值结果验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 动态批处理结合预条件能够在复杂设置下为BBVI提供严格的收敛保证，弥合了随机优化理论与实际BBVI实践之间的差距。

Abstract: Black-Box Variational Inference (BBVI) typically relies on Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) to optimize the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO). However, the stochastic gradients in BBVI inherently exhibit unbounded variance, violating standard assumptions and instead satisfying the weaker Blum-Gladyshev (BG) condition, where variance grows quadratically with distance from the optimum. In this paper, we bridge the gap between stochastic optimization theory and the practical instances of BBVI. Focusing on the broad elliptic location-scale family of parameterized distributions, we offer two main contributions. First, we prove the existence of an ELBO solution, a foundational property usually assumed a priori in the literature. Second, we establish comprehensive convergence guarantees spanning finite-time and asymptotic regimes for Minibatch Projected SGD (PSGD) equipped with dynamic batching and preconditioning under the BG condition. Our theoretical framework demonstrates that dynamic batching combined with preconditioning systematically enables rigorous guarantees even in complex settings. We illustrate our theoretical findings with numerical results, highlighting the efficacy of our approach for modern inference tasks.

</details>


### [323] [On the Invariance and Generality of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07546)
*Xing Han,Ziyin Liu,Suchi Saria,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了可泛化的缩放定律：在资源充足的源领域拟合一次后，可可靠地迁移到新领域。通过识别信息分辨率ρ这一关键不变量，证明缩放定律在双射（信息无损）变换下保持不变，而在非双射（信息有损）变换下可预测地变化，跨语言、视觉、语音任务验证，实现数据缩放指数误差在3%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律对新任务和领域的资源分配至关重要，但拟合缩放定律本身需要大量计算成本，这恰恰是缩放定律本来要节省的资源。因此需要可泛化的缩放定律——在资源充足的源领域拟合一次后，可迁移到新领域而无需重新完整扫描。

Method: 识别信息分辨率ρ作为关键不变量：缩放定律在双射（保信息）变换下保持不变；在非双射（降低信息分辨率）变换下，以信息论可预测的方式变化。跨语言、视觉、语音任务验证该理论。

Result: 成功实现两个跨领域应用：(1) 从通用文本拟合的缩放定律预测电子健康记录上语言模型的缩放行为；(2) 在不同噪声注入水平下预测时间序列分类的缩放，数据缩放指数误差在3%以内。

Conclusion: 缩放定律可以在不同领域间泛化迁移，关键在于理解信息分辨率ρ这一核心不变量，为资源有限的领域提供了实用的缩放预测方法。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws establish a predictable relationship between model performance and data or compute, offering crucial guidance for resource allocation in new domains and tasks. Yet such laws are most needed precisely where they are hardest to obtain: fitting one for a new model task pair demands expensive sweeps that typically exhaust the very compute budget the law is meant to economize. This paper poses the research question of how to develop generalizable scaling laws: laws fit once on a well-resourced source domain and reliably transported to new domains where running a full sweep is infeasible, which requires a fundamental understanding of when and why scaling properties change. We address this by identifying the right invariants: scaling laws are preserved under bijective (information-preserving) transformations of the data and modified in predictable, information-theoretically grounded ways under non-bijective transformations that lower its information resolution $ρ$: a single axis along which a law fit in one domain can be transported to another. We validate this across language, vision, and speech, and demonstrate two cross-domain applications: predicting scaling for language models trained on electronic health records from laws fit on general text, and predicting time-series classification scaling under varying levels of noise injection, recovering the data-scaling exponents to within $3\%$ error.

</details>


### [324] [Beyond Distribution Estimation: Simplex Anchored Structural Inference Towards Universal Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07557)
*Yaxin Hou,Jun Ma,Hanyang Li,Bo Han,Jie Yu,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对半监督学习中标注数据稀缺且未标注数据分布未知的通用半监督学习（UniSSL）场景，提出Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition（SAGE）方法。该方法绕过分布估计，通过捕捉样本间高阶依赖关系建立结构共识，利用单纯形等角紧框架引导表示分离，并结合分布无关的加权策略和辅助分支处理伪标签噪声。在五个基准数据集上SAGE平均准确率提升8.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习方法通常假设未标注数据服从均匀分布或需要大量标注数据估计分布，但在实际UniSSL场景中，未标注数据分布任意且未知，导致伪标签错误率高，引发表示混淆问题。因此需要一种不依赖分布估计、能有效利用未标注数据的方法。

Method: 1) 提出Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition (SAGE)，构建图结构捕获样本间高阶依赖关系，建立结构一致性来指导表示学习；2) 利用满足单纯形等角紧框架（simplex equiangular tight frame）的向量作为坐标帧，引导类间表示分离以缓解表示混淆；3) 设计基于分布无关度量的加权策略，优先选择可靠伪标签；4) 引入辅助分支隔离潜在的错误伪标签。

Result: 在五个标准基准数据集上的评估表明，SAGE方法一致性地优于现有最先进方法，平均准确率提升8.52%。

Conclusion: 在通用半监督学习场景下，聚焦于表示层面的结构推理（而非依赖分布估计）是更有效的策略。SAGE通过图结构共识、单纯形引导的表示分离以及伪标签质量控制，成功解决了UniSSL中的表示混淆问题，显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning faces significant challenges in realistic scenarios where labeled data is scarce and unlabeled data follows unknown, arbitrary distributions. We formalize this critical yet under-explored paradigm as Universal Semi-supervised Learning (UniSSL). Existing methods typically leverage unlabeled data via pseudo-labeling. However, they often rely on the idealized assumption of a uniform unlabeled data distribution or require sufficient labeled data to estimate it. In the UniSSL setting, such dependencies lead to numerous erroneous pseudo-labels, thereby triggering representation confusion. Fortunately, we observe that inter-sample relations captured by representations are more reliable than pseudo-labels. Leveraging this insight, we shift our focus to representation-level structural inference to bypass distribution estimation. Accordingly, we propose Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition (SAGE), which captures high-order inter-sample dependencies to establish structural consensus for guiding representation learning. Meanwhile, to mitigate representation confusion, we employ vectors that satisfy a simplex equiangular tight frame to serve as a coordinate frame for guiding inter-class representation separation. Finally, we introduce a weighting strategy based on distribution-agnostic metrics to prioritize reliable pseudo-labels and an auxiliary branch to isolate potentially erroneous pseudo-labels. Evaluations on five standard benchmarks show that SAGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with an average accuracy gain of \textbf{8.52\%}.

</details>


### [325] [Ensemble Distributionally Robust Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07565)
*Tigran Ramazyan,Denis Derkach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化算法，在连续上下文下保持计算可行性，获得了理论上的次线性遗憾界，改进了现有最优结果，且实验行为与理论保证一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在处理数据的复杂性和噪声时鲁棒性不足，现有的集成策略虽能缓解单一模型的弱点，但如何在对连续上下文保持计算可行的同时实现分布鲁棒性仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出了一种新的集成分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化算法，能够在连续上下文设置下保持计算可处理性，并结合理论分析推导了次线性遗憾界。

Result: 获得了理论上的次线性遗憾界，改进了当前最优结果，且实验行为与理论保证一致。

Conclusion: 该算法在理论上优于现有方法，且实证结果验证了理论分析的正确性，为分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化提供了可行的集成方案。

Abstract: We study zeroth-order optimisation under context distributional uncertainty, a setting commonly tackled using Bayesian optimisation (BO). A prevailing strategy to make BO more robust to the complex and noisy nature of data is to employ an ensemble as the surrogate model, thereby mitigating the weaknesses of any single model. In this study, we propose a novel algorithm for Ensemble Distributionally Robust Bayesian Optimisation that remains computationally tractable while managing continuous context. We obtain theoretical sublinear regret bounds, improving current state-of-the-art results. We show that our method's empirical behaviour aligns with its theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [326] [Bilevel Graph Structure Learning, Revisited: Inner-Channel Origins of the Reported Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07577)
*Minkyoung Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现双层图结构学习中的性能提升很大程度上来自内循环训练动态（隐式梯度正则化），而非图结构重连本身。作者提出frozen-φ控制实验来分解增益来源，并验证了该发现在时空流预测和节点分类任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为双层图结构学习通过联合优化模型参数和学到的图结构来提升GNN性能，并将增益归因于重连后的邻接矩阵。作者对此提出质疑，希望厘清性能提升的真正来源是结构学习本身还是训练动态效应。

Method: 提出frozen-φ控制方法，冻结学到的图结构同时保留内循环训练调度，从而将双层增益分解为：（1）内通道——T步训练动态与隐式梯度正则化；（2）图通道——图结构重连本身。此外提出图蒸馏作为方法无关的补充，以及三先验条件框架用于预测增益符号。

Result: 在时空流预测任务上，内通道贡献了78-101%的增益；在节点分类任务上（伯努利边级参数化），内通道贡献了37-44%的增益。经典谱诊断指标与任务增益可以解耦。三先验条件框架在全部六个基准上成功预测了双层增益的符号。

Conclusion: 双层图结构学习的性能提升主要来源于内循环训练动态效应，而非图结构重连本身。作者建议将frozen-φ作为标准化诊断工具，并提出三先验条件框架用于预测增益方向。

Abstract: Bilevel graph structure learning is widely understood to improve graph neural networks by jointly optimizing model parameters and a learned graph structure, with the resulting performance gain attributed to the rewired adjacency. We find that this attribution may be overstated: training-dynamics effects in the inner loop, rather than the rewiring itself, capture a substantial share of the gain. To establish this, we introduce frozen-$φ$, a control that freezes the graph while retaining the inner-loop training schedule. This decomposes the bilevel gain into an inner channel of $T$-step training dynamics with implicit gradient regularization and a graph channel of the graph rewiring itself. On spatio-temporal flow forecasting the inner channel matches or exceeds the full bilevel pipeline, accounting for 78-101% of the gain; on node classification it accounts for 37-44% under a Bernoulli edge-level parameterization. We also verify that classical spectral diagnostics can dissociate from task gain. We propose frozen-$φ$ as a standardized diagnostic for bilevel graph structure learning, with graph distillation as a method-agnostic complement. A three-precondition framework further predicts the sign of the bilevel gain on all six benchmarks.

</details>


### [327] [Revisiting Transformer Layer Parameterization Through Causal Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07588)
*Jin Xu,Camille Couturier,Victor Rühle,Saravan Rajmohan,James Hensman*
